but seriously, it’s all yours.


Dependency inventory scope: installed packages in the package-lock.json production
dependency graph. This is a conservative superset: build-time and tree-shaken code
may not appear in the final browser bundle. Platform packages are listed only for
the platform installed during notice generation. Shared license texts are deduplicated
by SHA-256; each package below points to the full retained text(s). Where a package
omits its license file, a same-project license reference is identified explicitly.
Repository references for omitted license files record the exact retrieved commit.

Cleanup reconciliation: all original inventory entries and full license texts
are retained as a conservative superset. Linux build-tool package entries
are added from this verification platform, with explicit same-project license
references where platform packages omit their own files. Every installed
production package/version is covered. Remaining versions and integrity
hashes are unchanged. Six new optional WASI lock entries were verified inside
the same hash-checked Tailwind Oxide archive, not independently upgraded.
Original location fields describe the source inventory.
